                  - generic [ref=e5993]: 13 days ago, I got to know about Hexa Health, and I'm really glad I reached out to them. Based on the doctor's guidance, my mother gone for a hip replacement surgery at Divyakirti Hospital and the entire experience was well managed.ensuring everything went smoothly and comfortably.       - heading "Real Patients, Real Stories" [level=2] [ref=e6512]
      - paragraph [ref=e6513]: Experience how HexaHealth transformed treatment journeys with trust, and care into successful recovery stories.
      - generic [ref=e6515]:
        - generic [ref=e6519]:
          - paragraph [ref=e6520]: Rajesh Sharma
          - paragraph [ref=e6522]: I got connected with HexaHealth and they did everything on my behalf. Even when I got admitted to the hospital, they were there for me like my brother, and I did not need anybody else do things for me. It was a very seamless process which HexaHealth have done for me.
        - generic [ref=e6526]:
          - paragraph [ref=e6527]: Anupama
          - generic [ref=e6528]:
            - paragraph [ref=e6529]: Baaki jagah par laser ke naam par open surgery karte the. Lekin, mujhe laga ki laser advanced technology hai, ismein pain bhi kam hoga. Laser surgery ke 2-3 options mein se humne HexaHealth ke saath jaana decide kiya kyunki sabse achi baat jo mujhe lagi - HexaHealth Aapko Freedom Deta Hai Doctor Choose Karne Ki.
            - paragraph [ref=e6530]: ...Read More
        - generic [ref=e6534]:
          - paragraph [ref=e6535]: Siddhant
          - generic [ref=e6536]:
            - paragraph [ref=e6537]: Mujhe HexaHealth ke baare mein online pata chala. HexaHealth ne ek Plastic Surgeon, Dr. Priyanka Sharma, se connect karvaya. It was very good aur main suggest karunga agar aap log bhi Surgery kara rahe ho toh HexaHealth ke through hi karao. Main toh kaafi khush hun unki sabhi services se.
            - paragraph [ref=e6538]: ...Read More
        - generic [ref=e6542]:
          - paragraph [ref=e6543]: Madhu
          - paragraph [ref=e6545]: Karib 2-3 saal se Hernia se pareshaan thi. Kisi relative ne mujhe HexaHealth ke baare mein bataya aur main online appointment book kari. Agar koi bhi aage kisi ko dikkat aati hai toh main unhe HexaHealth ke baare mein bataungi. HexaHealth kaafi helpful hai aur apne budget ke andar hai.
        - generic [ref=e6549]:
          - paragraph [ref=e6550]: Meharban
          - paragraph [ref=e6552]: Koi dikkat, koi pareshani nahi hui. HexaHealth ne poori help kari. Humein har jagah attend karte rahe. Bahut hi accha experience raha.
